Experiments across diverse human-scene datasets show that IMU-to-4D yields more coherent and temporally stable results than SoTA cascaded pipelines, suggesting wearable motion sensors alone can support rich 4D understanding.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Design, Modelling and Experimental Evaluation of a Tendon-driven Wrist Abduction-Adduction Mechanism for an upper limb exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20893)
*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于手腕外展/内收的单缆绳扭簧辅助驱动机构，通过仿真方法优化弹簧刚度参数，并制作原型在五名无运动障碍参与者上验证，结果表明仿真指导的设计可有效减少经验调参依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手腕外骨骼驱动机制（电机、气动）存在重量大、摩擦高、结构复杂等缺点，需要更轻量化、简洁的驱动方案。

Method: 采用单根Bowden缆绳配合螺旋扭簧（时钟弹簧）被动张紧的单缆驱动机制，建立运动学与动力学模型估算所需扭矩并优化弹簧参数，基于仿真结果制作原型，并在五种弹簧配置下进行实验验证。

Result: 实验与仿真趋势一致，标称弹簧配置在运动范围、扭矩需求和重复性之间取得平衡，证明了仿真指导的刚度选择方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 仿真驱动的刚度参数选择可有效指导紧凑型缆绳驱动手腕外骨骼的设计，减少对经验调参的依赖。

Abstract: Wrist exoskeletons play a vital role in rehabilitation and assistive applications, yet conventional actuation mechanisms such as electric motors or pneumatics often introduce undesirable weight, friction, and complexity. This paper presents a novel single-cable (tendon), torsional-spring-assisted actuation mechanism for wrist abduction-adduction, and a simulation-based method for selecting its stiffness parameters. The mechanism employs a single Bowden cable passively tensioned by a spiral torsional spring (clock spring) to maintain continuous cable tension without antagonistic actuation. Kinematic and dynamic modeling of the mechanism was performed to estimate the required torque and identify optimal spring parameters. These simulation-derived parameters guided the design of a functional prototype, which was experimentally evaluated with five participants with no motor disabilities (NMD) under varying arm positions and loading conditions using three spring configurations to account for user variability and modeling uncertainties. Experimental results show consistent agreement with simulation-derived trends, with the nominal spring configuration achieving balanced motion range, torque demand, and repeatability. The results demonstrate that simulation-informed stiffness selection can effectively guide the design of compact, cable-driven wrist exoskeletons while reducing reliance on empirical tuning.

</details>


### [73] [A Tendon-Driven Wrist Abduction-Adduction Joint Improves Performance of a 5 DoF Upper Limb Exoskeleton -- Implementation and Experimental Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20898)
*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,Alexander L. Ammitzbøll,Ellen-Merete Hagen,Jakob Blicher,Izabella Obál,Ana S. S. Cardoso,Oguzhan Kirtas,Rasmus L. Kæseler,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在五自由度上肢外骨骼EXOTIC2中集成主动腕部外展-内收（Ab-Ad）关节对日常生活活动功能的影响。通过随机对照实验（8名健康受试者+1名ALS患者初步测试），发现腕部Ab-Ad辅助显著改善了任务成功率：饮水任务溢漏率从56%降至3%，搔抓任务水平对齐成功率从28%提升至75%，且未增加执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕关节功能对日常生活活动至关重要，但现有研究缺乏关于上肢外骨骼中主动腕部Ab-Ad关节辅助功能影响的实验证据，尤其针对严重运动障碍患者的康复需求。

Method: 设计了紧凑轻量化的肌腱驱动外展+弹簧驱动内收的腕部模块，集成到EXOTIC外骨骼中。采用随机对照实验设计，在腕部解锁和锁定两种条件下，让8名健康受试者完成饮水和搔抓任务，并对1名ALS患者进行初步可行性测试，评估运动学和任务表现指标。

Result: 腕部Ab-Ad自由度辅助显著改善了任务成功指标：饮水任务溢漏发生率从56%降至3%，搔抓任务水平对齐成功率从28%提升至75%，且任务完成时间未增加。

Conclusion: 集成腕部Ab-Ad辅助可显著改善外骨骼辅助下日常生活活动的关键功能任务表现，为主动腕部Ab-Ad控制增强外骨骼辅助ADL任务表现提供了实验证据。

Abstract: Wrist function is essential in performing activities of daily living (ADLs). However, there is limited experimental evidence on the functional impact of wrist Abduction-Adduction (Ab-Ad) joint assistance in upper limb exoskeletons (ULEs) for rehabilitation. This study evaluates the effect of implementing an active wrist Ab-Ad joint in a five degree of freedom (DoF) ULE, EXOTIC2 exoskeleton, to support individuals with severe motor impairments. Methods: A compact, lightweight wrist module with tendon-driven abduction and spring-driven adduction was integrated into the EXOTIC exoskeleton. Eight adults with no motor disabilities completed drinking and scratching tasks under randomized wrist-enabled and wrist-locked conditions along with a preliminary feasibility test in one individual with Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS). Kinematic and task performance metrics including wrist range of motion, task completion time, spillage and leveling metrics were assessed. Results: Implementing the wrist Ab-Ad DoF improved task success metrics. Spill incidence during the drinking task decreased from 56% to 3%, and leveling success for scratching task improved from 28% to 75%. Conclusion: Integrating wrist Ab-Ad assistance improved key functional task outcomes without increasing execution time. Significance: The study provides the experimental evidence that active wrist Ab-Ad control enhances task-level performance in exoskeleton-assisted ADLs.

</details>


### [74] [Clinical Evaluation of a Tongue-Controlled Wrist Abduction-Adduction Assistance in a 6-DoF Upper-Limb Exoskeleton for Individuals with ALS and SCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20967)
*Juwairiya S. Khan,Mostafa Mohammadi,Alexander L. Ammitzbøll,Ellen-Merete Hagen,Jakob Blicher Izabella Obál,Ana S. S. Cardoso,Oguzhan Kirtas,Rasmus L. Kæseler,John Rasmussen,Lotte N. S. Andreasen Struijk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了舌控6自由度上肢外骨骼（EXOTIC2）中腕部外展-内收（Ab-Ad）辅助对运动功能障碍患者日常活动的影响。结果表明，增加腕部Ab-Ad辅助显著提高了任务成功率（溢洒从77.8%降至22.2%，放置失败从66.7%降至16.7%），且未增加不适感，但效果取决于任务情境和个体策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上肢外骨骼有潜力恢复严重运动障碍患者的功能独立性，但腕部自由度（特别是外展-内收Ab-Ad）的临床相关性尚未得到充分评估。现有研究对腕部Ab-Ad辅助在实际日常活动中的功能价值和用户体验缺乏系统证据。

Method: 在1名肌萎缩侧索硬化症患者和5名脊髓损伤患者中开展受试者内研究，使用舌控6自由度上肢外骨骼EXOTIC2，设置有无腕部Ab-Ad辅助两种条件，执行饮水与刮痒棒调平两项日常活动任务。测量指标包括任务成功率、完成时间、运动学数据及可用性问卷（舒适度、功能感知、接受度）。

Result: 启用腕部Ab-Ad辅助后，两项日常活动的任务成功率均提升：溢洒率从77.8%降至22.2%，放置失败率从66.7%降至16.7%。受试者利用任务特定的腕部运动范围子集，表明功能范围内的有效控制比最大关节活动范围更关键。问卷结果显示额外自由度未增加不适感，且受试者感知到任务表现提升。

Conclusion: 腕部Ab-Ad辅助在辅助性外骨骼使用中能增强功能性任务表现，且不影响用户舒适度。但其效果依赖于任务情境、控制可用性和个体用户策略。该研究为在上肢外骨骼中纳入腕部Ab-Ad提供了以用户为中心的临床证据，强调辅助设备设计中功能能力与可用性的平衡。

Abstract: Upper-limb exoskeletons (ULEs) have the potential to restore functional independence in individuals with severe motor impairments; however, the clinical relevance of wrist degrees of freedom (DoF), particularly abduction-adduction (Ab-Ad), remains insufficiently evaluated. This study investigates the functional and user-perceived impact of wrist Ab-Ad assistance during two activities of daily living (ADLs). Wrist Ab-Ad assistance in a tongue-controlled 6-DoF ULE, EXOTIC2, was evaluated in a within-subject study involving one individual with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis and five individuals with spinal cord injury. Participants performed drinking and scratch stick leveling tasks with EXOTIC2 under two conditions: with and without wrist Ab-Ad assistance. Outcome measure included task success, task completion time, kinematic measures, and a usability questionnaire capturing comfort, functional perception, and acceptance. Enabling wrist Ab-Ad improved task success rates across both ADLs, with consistent reductions in spillage (from 77.8% spillages to 22.2%) and failed placements (from 66.7% to 16.7%). Participants utilized task-specific subsets of the available wrist range of motion, indicating that effective control within functional ranges was more critical than maximal joint excursion. Questionnaire responses indicated no increase in discomfort with the additional DoF and reflected perceived improvements in task performance. In conclusion, wrist Ab-Ad assistance enhances functional task performance in assistive exoskeleton use without compromising user comfort. However, its effectiveness depends on task context, control usability, and individual user strategies. This study provides clinically relevant, user-centered evidence supporting the inclusion of wrist Ab-Ad in ULEs, emphasizing the importance of balancing functional capability with usability in assistive device design.

</details>


### [75] [A Survey of Legged Robotics in Non-Inertial Environments: Past, Present, and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20990)
*I-Chia Chang,Xinyan Huang,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Sangli Teng,Wenjing Li,Maani Ghaffari,Jingang Yi,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了腿式机器人在非惯性环境（如移动、倾斜或加速的地面）中的建模、状态估计与控制方法，分析了性能退化原因，总结了现有方法的假设与局限，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在刚性静止地面已展现出卓越灵活性，但在非惯性环境（如地面运输、海洋平台、航空航天场景）中，地面运动引入的时变扰动破坏了传统静止地面假设，导致运动可靠性严重受限。现有方法缺乏对这一系统性问题的全面梳理。

Method: 通过系统性综述的方式，总结代表性应用领域与运动特性，分析腿式机器人性能退化的根本原因，回顾现有建模、状态估计与控制方法及其关键假设与局限性。

Result: 识别出机器人-环境耦合、可观测性、鲁棒性和实验验证等方面的开放问题，总结该领域的现有技术基础与瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该综述明确了非惯性环境下腿式机器人技术的理论基础，为开发适用于真实动态环境的可靠腿式机器人提供了方向指引，包括自主性、系统级设计、仿生策略、安全性与测试等未来方向。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated remarkable agility on rigid, stationary ground, but their locomotion reliability remains limited in non-inertial environments, where the supporting ground moves, tilts, or accelerates. Such conditions arise in ground transportation, maritime platforms, and aerospace settings, and they introduce persistent time-varying disturbances that break the stationary-ground assumptions underlying conventional legged locomotion. This survey reviews the state of the art in modeling, state estimation, and control for legged robots in non-inertial environments. We summarize representative application domains and motion characteristics, analyze the root causes of locomotion performance degradation, and review existing methods together with their key assumptions and limitations. We further identify open problems in robot-environment coupling, observability, robustness, and experimental validation, and discuss future directions in autonomy, system-level design, bio-inspired strategies, safety, and testing. The survey aims to clarify the technical foundations of this emerging area and support the development of reliable legged robots for real-world dynamic environments.

</details>

Huver,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Open-H-Embodiment——迄今最大的开源医疗机器人视频与运动学数据集，覆盖49+机构、多种机器人平台，并基于该数据集训练了两个基础模型：首个开源医疗机器人视觉-语言-动作模型GR00T-H（在结构化缝合基准中达成25%端到端成功率，其他模型为0%）和首个动作条件世界模型Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator（支持9种机器人平台的仿真与策略评估），证明了大规模开源医疗机器人数据对推动基础模型研究的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗机器人数据集规模小、单一本体、很少公开共享，严重制约了医疗机器人领域基础模型的发展，亟需大规模、多平台、开源的数据基础设施。

Method: 收集来自49+机构、涵盖CMR Versius、da Vinci、dVRK、BiTrack、MIRA、Maestro等多种机器人平台的医疗机器人视频与同步运动学数据，构建Open-H-Embodiment数据集；在此基础上训练GR00T-H（视觉-语言-动作模型）和Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator（动作条件世界模型）。

Result: GR00T-H在结构化缝合基准上实现25%端到端任务完成率（其他模型均为0%），在29步离体缝合序列上平均成功率达64%；Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator首次实现从单一检查点支持9种机器人平台的多本体手术仿真。

Conclusion: 开放、大规模医疗机器人数据收集可作为研究社区的关键基础设施，推动机器人学习和世界建模等领域的进步。

Abstract: Autonomous medical robots hold promise to improve patient outcomes, reduce provider workload, democratize access to care, and enable superhuman precision. However, autonomous medical robotics has been limited by a fundamental data problem: existing medical robotic datasets are small, single-embodiment, and rarely shared openly, restricting the development of foundation models that the field needs to advance. We introduce Open-H-Embodiment, the largest open dataset of medical robotic video with synchronized kinematics to date, spanning more than 49 institutions and multiple robotic platforms including the CMR Versius, Intuitive Surgical's da Vinci, da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK), Rob Surgical BiTrack, Virtual Incision's MIRA, Moon Surgical Maestro, and a variety of custom systems, spanning surgical manipulation, robotic ultrasound, and endoscopy procedures. We demonstrate the research enabled by this dataset through two foundation models. GR00T-H is the first open foundation vision-language-action model for medical robotics, which is the only evaluated model to achieve full end-to-end task completion on a structured suturing benchmark (25% of trials vs. 0% for all others) and achieves 64% average success across a 29-step ex vivo suturing sequence. We also train Cosmos-H-Surgical-Simulator, the first action-conditioned world model to enable multi-embodiment surgical simulation from a single checkpoint, spanning nine robotic platforms and supporting in silico policy evaluation and synthetic data generation for the medical domain. These results suggest that open, large-scale medical robot data collection can serve as critical infrastructure for the research community, enabling advances in robot learning, world modeling, and beyond.

</details>


### [77] [Neuro-Symbolic Manipulation Understanding with Enriched Semantic Event Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21053)
*Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出eSEC-LAM，一个将经典语义事件链(eSECs)转化为显式事件级符号状态的神经符号框架，通过置信度感知谓词、功能对象角色、先验知识、原始级抽象和显著性解释线索来增强符号状态，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100等数据集上实现了有竞争力的动作识别和显著提升的下一个原始动作预测，并在感知噪声下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的增强语义事件链(eSECs)虽然提供了可解释的关系性操作描述，但本质上仍是描述性的，无法直接支持不确定性感知的决策，缺乏处理感知噪声和进行概率推理的能力。

Method: 提出eSEC-LAM框架：1) 基于基础模型的感知前端进行确定性谓词提取；2) 将eSECs转化为包含置信度感知谓词、功能对象角色、affordance先验、原始级抽象和显著性解释线索的显式符号状态；3) 通过轻量级符号推理在原始操作的前置/后置条件上进行当前动作推理和下一个原始动作预测。

Result: 在EPIC-KITCHENS-100、EPIC-KITCHENS VISOR和Assembly101上的实验表明：eSEC-LAM在动作识别上具有竞争力，大幅提升了下一个原始动作预测，在感知退化条件下比经典符号方法和端到端视频基线更鲁棒，并能提供基于显式关系证据的时间一致性解释轨迹。

Conclusion: 增强语义事件链不仅可以作为可解释的操作描述符，还可以作为神经符号动作推理的有效内部状态表示。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in human environments must reason about how object interactions evolve over time, which actions are currently being performed, and what manipulation step is likely to follow. Classical enriched Semantic Event Chains (eSECs) provide an interpretable relational description of manipulation, but remain primarily descriptive and do not directly support uncertainty-aware decision making. In this paper, we propose eSEC-LAM, a neuro-symbolic framework that transforms eSECs into an explicit event-level symbolic state for manipulation understanding. The proposed formulation augments classical eSECs with confidence-aware predicates, functional object roles, affordance priors, primitive-level abstraction, and saliency-guided explanation cues. These enriched symbolic states are derived from a foundation-model-based perception front-end through deterministic predicate extraction, while current-action inference and next-primitive prediction are performed using lightweight symbolic reasoning over primitive pre- and post-conditions. We evaluate the proposed framework on EPIC-KITCHENS-100, EPIC-KITCHENS VISOR, and Assembly101 across action recognition, next-primitive prediction, robustness to perception noise, and explanation consistency. Experimental results show that eSEC-LAM achieves competitive action recognition, substantially improves next-primitive prediction, remains more robust under degraded perceptual conditions than both classical symbolic and end-to-end video baselines, and provides temporally consistent explanation traces grounded in explicit relational evidence. These findings demonstrate that enriched Semantic Event Chains can serve not only as interpretable descriptors of manipulation, but also as effective internal states for neuro-symbolic action reasoning.

</details>


### [78] [Impact-Aware Model Predictive Control for UAV Landing on a Heaving Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21078)
*Jess Stephenson,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于牛顿恢复定律和线性互补问题（LCP）的冲击感知模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于无人机在起伏海上平台着陆时抑制反弹；仿真中降低了撞击前相对速度并提高了鲁棒性，实验中相比跟踪MPC减少了86.2%的撞击后偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在起伏海上平台着陆时，由于垂直相对运动会产生巨大冲击力并导致反弹，现有方法对此问题处理不足。

Method: 将着陆建模为速度级刚体碰撞（牛顿恢复定律），以线性互补问题（LCP）嵌入MPC动力学中，预测不连续的碰撞后速度并抑制反弹。

Result: 仿真中恢复率感知的预测降低了撞击前相对速度并提升了着陆鲁棒性；实验中在起伏甲板测试台相比跟踪MPC减少了86.2%的撞击后偏移。

Conclusion: 冲击感知MPC能有效抑制无人机在起伏平台上着陆时的反弹，显著提升着陆性能。

Abstract: Landing UAVs on heaving marine platforms is challenging because relative vertical motion can generate large impact forces and cause rebound on touchdown. To address this, we develop an impact-aware Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework that models landing as a velocity-level rigid-body impact governed by Newton's restitution law. We embed this as a linear complementarity problem (LCP) within the MPC dynamics to predict the discontinuous post-impact velocity and suppress rebound. In simulation, restitution-aware prediction reduces pre-impact relative velocity and improves landing robustness. Experiments on a heaving-deck testbed show an 86.2% reduction in post-impact deflection compared to a tracking MPC.

</details>


### [79] [Self-Predictive Representation for Autonomous UAV Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21130)
*Angel Ayala,Donling Sui,Francisco Cruz,Mitchell Torok,Mohammad Deghat,Bruno J. T. Fernandes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为AmelPred的自预测模型（及其随机版本AmelPredSto），用于解决3D目标导航任务中无人机强化学习的数据样本效率问题。实验表明，AmelPredSto与actor-critic算法结合时表现最佳，显著提升了RL算法的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在目标导航中面临两大挑战：1）强化学习算法数据样本利用效率低下；2）在未知目标位置设定下的目标识别问题。现有方法大多使用坐标导航而非直接寻找目标，缺乏对状态表征学习与无模型RL结合的系统分析。

Method: 1) 将未知目标位置设定形式化为马尔可夫决策过程；2) 开发新型自预测模型AmelPred作为感知模块；3) 系统分析不同状态表征学习方法与无模型RL算法的交互；4) 提出随机版本AmelPredSto。

Result: AmelPredSto是性能最优的状态表征学习模型；与actor-critic RL算法结合时，在解决目标导航问题上显著提升了RL算法的效率。

Conclusion: 本研究通过创新的自预测感知模型有效缓解了数据样本效率问题，为无人机在3D环境中的自主目标导航提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have revolutionized industries through their versatility with applications including aerial surveillance, search and rescue, agriculture, and delivery. Their autonomous capabilities offer unique advantages, such as operating in large open space environments. Reinforcement Learning (RL) empowers UAVs to learn intricate navigation policies, enabling them to optimize flight behavior autonomously. However, one of its main challenge is the inefficiency in using data sample to achieve a good policy. In object-goal navigation (OGN) settings, target recognition arises as an extra challenge. Most UAV-related approaches use relative or absolute coordinates to move from an initial position to a predefined location, rather than to find the target directly. This study addresses the data sample efficiency issue in solving a 3D OGN problem, in addition to, the formalization of the unknown target location setting as a Markov decision process. Experiments are conducted to analyze the interplay of different state representation learning (SRL) methods for perception with a model-free RL algorithm for planning in an autonomous navigation system. The main contribution of this study is the development of the perception module, featuring a novel self-predictive model named AmelPred. Empirical results demonstrate that its stochastic version, AmelPredSto, is the best-performing SRL model when combined with actor-critic RL algorithms. The obtained results show substantial improvement in RL algorithms' efficiency by using AmelPredSto in solving the OGN problem.

</details>


### [80] [Navigating the Clutter: Waypoint-Based Bi-Level Planning for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21138)
*Jiabao Ji,Yongchao Chen,Yang Zhang,Ramana Rao Kompella,Chuchu Fan,Gaowen Liu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合多机器人控制框架，通过引入航点表示和基于课程学习的RLVR算法，联合优化任务规划与运动规划，在密集障碍物环境（最多9台机器人）中显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在杂乱环境中的控制面临机器人间碰撞、机器人-障碍物碰撞及不可达运动等复杂物理约束，需要联合优化高层任务规划与低层运动规划，但两者联合优化因运动轨迹参数化复杂和跨层级信用分配模糊而困难。

Method: 提出混合多机器人控制框架：1）引入航点作为运动轨迹的简洁表达方式以实现有效参数化；2）采用基于课程学习的改进RLVR算法，将运动规划器的运动可行性反馈传播给任务规划器，解决信用分配难题。

Result: 在密集障碍物、最多九台机器人的BoxNet3D-OBS多机器人基准测试中，该方法在任务成功率上持续优于不考虑运动的基线方法和基于VLA的基线方法。

Conclusion: 航点表示与课程学习式RLVR算法的结合能有效实现任务与运动规划的联合优化，在复杂多机器人环境中表现优越。

Abstract: Multi-robot control in cluttered environments is a challenging problem that involves complex physical constraints, including robot-robot collisions, robot-obstacle collisions, and unreachable motions. Successful planning in such settings requires joint optimization over high-level task planning and low-level motion planning, as violations of physical constraints may arise from failures at either level. However, jointly optimizing task and motion planning is difficult due to the complex parameterization of low-level motion trajectories and the ambiguity of credit assignment across the two planning levels. In this paper, we propose a hybrid multi-robot control framework that jointly optimizes task and motion planning. To enable effective parameterization of low-level planning, we introduce waypoints, a simple yet expressive representation for motion trajectories. To address the credit assignment challenge, we adopt a curriculum-based training strategy with a modified RLVR algorithm that propagates motion feasibility feedback from the motion planner to the task planner. Experiments on BoxNet3D-OBS, a challenging multi-robot benchmark with dense obstacles and up to nine robots, show that our approach consistently improves task success over motion-agnostic and VLA-based baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/UCSB-NLP-Chang/navigate-cluster

</details>


### [81] [Full-Body Dynamic Safety for Robot Manipulators: 3D Poisson Safety Functions for CBF-Based Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21189)
*Meg Wilkinson,Gilbert Bahati,Ryan M. Bena,Emily Fourney,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D泊松安全函数（PSF）的框架，用于机械臂在动态环境中的全身碰撞避免，通过将自由空间进行缓冲处理并求解泊松方程生成全局平滑的控制屏障函数（CBF），并在7自由度机械臂上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械臂的碰撞避免需要在高维构型空间中强制执行全身安全约束，而基于CBF的安全滤波器虽然有效，但处理大量约束时面临理论和计算上的挑战。

Method: 利用3D泊松安全函数（PSF），对环境占用数据进行采样，通过Pontryagin差根据采样分辨率收缩自由空间；在缓冲域上求解泊松方程合成全局平滑的CBF；通过多约束二次规划实现实时安全滤波器。

Result: 证明了在缓冲区域保持采样点安全即可保证整个连续机器人表面的碰撞避免，并在7自由度机械臂动态环境中验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了高维构型空间中全身碰撞避免的理论和计算挑战，实现了动态环境下的实时安全控制。

Abstract: Collision avoidance for robotic manipulators requires enforcing full-body safety constraints in high-dimensional configuration spaces. Control Barrier Function (CBF) based safety filters have proven effective in enabling safe behaviors, but enforcing the high number of constraints needed for safe manipulation leads to theoretic and computational challenges. This work presents a framework for full-body collision avoidance for manipulators in dynamic environments by leveraging 3D Poisson Safety Functions (PSFs). In particular, given environmental occupancy data, we sample the manipulator surface at a prescribed resolution and shrink free space via a Pontryagin difference according to this resolution. On this buffered domain, we synthesize a globally smooth CBF by solving Poisson's equation, yielding a single safety function for the entire environment. This safety function, evaluated at each sampled point, yields task-space CBF constraints enforced by a real-time safety filter via a multi-constraint quadratic program. We prove that keeping the sample points safe in the buffered region guarantees collision avoidance for the entire continuous robot surface. The framework is validated on a 7-degree-of-freedom manipulator in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [82] [How VLAs (Really) Work In Open-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21192)
*Amir Rasouli,Yangzheng Wu,Zhiyuan Li,Rui Heng Yang,Xuan Zhao,Charles Eret,Sajjad Pakdamansavoji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出现有VLA模型在BEHAVIOR1K基准上的评估仅关注最终成功率而忽略安全性和过程一致性，提出包含安全违规检测的新评估协议，并分析了当前模型的鲁棒性、安全性和任务意识等不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估协议仅基于最终状态的成功率或部分得分来衡量模型性能，忽略了操作过程中的安全问题和事件一致性，可能夸大实际性能，阻碍机器人策略在真实场景中的安全部署。

Method: 对B1K挑战中的先进VLA模型进行全面分析，从鲁棒性（可重复性与一致性）、安全性、任务意识以及任务未完成的关键因素等维度进行评估，并提出能够捕获安全违规的新评估协议。

Result: 发现现有评估指标会夸大模型的实际表现；当前VLA模型在安全性、一致性和任务意识方面存在显著不足；提出的新评估协议能够更真实地反映策略在复杂交互场景中的性能。

Conclusion: 必须改进评估标准以纳入安全性指标，现有VLA模型在鲁棒性和安全性方面存在局限，需推动未来研究朝向更安全、更可靠的机器人操作策略发展。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have been extensively used in robotics applications, achieving great success in various manipulation problems. More recently, VLAs have been used in long-horizon tasks and evaluated on benchmarks, such as BEHAVIOR1K (B1K), for solving complex household chores. The common metric for measuring progress in such benchmarks is success rate or partial score based on satisfaction of progress-agnostic criteria, meaning only the final states of the objects are considered, regardless of the events that lead to such states. In this paper, we argue that using such evaluation protocols say little about safety aspects of operation and can potentially exaggerate reported performance, undermining core challenges for future real-world deployment. To this end, we conduct a thorough analysis of state-of-the-art models on the B1K Challenge and evaluate policies in terms of robustness via reproducibility and consistency of performance, safety aspects of policies operations, task awareness, and key elements leading to the incompletion of tasks. We then propose evaluation protocols to capture safety violations to better measure the true performance of the policies in more complex and interactive scenarios. At the end, we discuss the limitations of the existing VLAs and motivate future research.

</details>


### [83] [CorridorVLA: Explicit Spatial Constraints for Generative Action Heads via Sparse Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21241)
*Dachong Li,ZhuangZhuang Chen,Jin Zhang,Jianqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CorridorVLA，通过预测稀疏空间锚点（Δ-位置）定义显式容差走廊，引导流匹配动作头生成动作，在LIBERO-Plus基准上提升成功率3.4%-12.4%，GR00T-Corr变体达83.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通过隐式潜在特征注入空间引导，缺乏显式、可解释的空间约束来指导动作生成。

Method: 预测稀疏空间锚点作为增量物理变化（Δ-位置），在训练目标中定义显式容差走廊（corridor），流匹配动作头根据轨迹是否落在走廊外施加矫正梯度，同时允许微小偏差和噪声。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus基准上，SmolVLA和GR00T的成功率分别提升3.4%-12.4%；GR00T-Corr变体达83.21%。

Conclusion: 动作对齐的物理线索可为生成式动作策略提供直接、可解释的约束，补充视觉或隐式编码的空间引导。

Abstract: Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models often use intermediate representations to connect multimodal inputs with continuous control, yet spatial guidance is often injected implicitly through latent features. We propose $CorridorVLA$, which predicts sparse spatial anchors as incremental physical changes (e.g., $Δ$-positions) and uses them to impose an explicit tolerance region in the training objective for action generation. The anchors define a corridor that guides a flow-matching action head: trajectories whose implied spatial evolution falls outside it receive corrective gradients, while minor deviations from contacts and execution noise are permitted. On the more challenging LIBERO-Plus benchmark, CorridorVLA yields consistent gains across both SmolVLA and GR00T, improving success rate by $3.4\%$--$12.4\%$ over the corresponding baselines; notably, our GR00T-Corr variant reaches a success rate of $83.21\%$. These results indicate that action-aligned physical cues can provide direct and interpretable constraints for generative action policies, complementing spatial guidance encoded in visual or latent forms. Code is available at https://github.com/corridorVLA.

</details>


### [84] [Reasoning About Traversability: Language-Guided Off-Road 3D Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21249)
*Byounggun Park,Soonmin Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对越野自动驾驶中视觉语言模型(VLM)的标注与车辆动作、地形几何弱对齐问题，提出语言精炼框架将标注重构为动作对齐对，结合偏好优化策略引入几何感知硬负样本惩罚与高程不一致的轨迹，在ORAD-3D基准上实现轨迹误差从1.01m降至0.97m，可通行性合规从0.621提升至0.644，高程不一致从0.428降至0.322。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越野数据集的语言标注与车辆动作和地形几何弱对齐，导致VLM无法准确理解场景与规划合理轨迹。

Method: 1) 语言精炼框架：将标注重构为动作对齐对，使VLM从单张图像生成场景描述和3D未来轨迹；2) 偏好优化策略：构建几何感知硬负样本，显式惩罚与局部高程轮廓不一致的轨迹；3) 提出越野专用评估指标：可通行性合规度与高程一致性。

Result: 在ORAD-3D基准上，平均轨迹误差从1.01m降至0.97m，可通行性合规从0.621提升至0.644，高程不一致从0.428降至0.322。

Conclusion: 动作对齐监督和地形感知优化策略能有效提升越野场景下VLM的轨迹规划鲁棒性，验证了语言精炼与偏好优化在越野自动驾驶中的有效性。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable high-level semantic reasoning for end-to-end autonomous driving, particularly in unstructured environments, existing off-road datasets suffer from language annotations that are weakly aligned with vehicle actions and terrain geometry. To address this misalignment, we propose a language refinement framework that restructures annotations into action-aligned pairs, enabling a VLM to generate refined scene descriptions and 3D future trajectories directly from a single image. To further encourage terrain-aware planning, we introduce a preference optimization strategy that constructs geometry-aware hard negatives and explicitly penalizes trajectories inconsistent with local elevation profiles. Furthermore, we propose off-road-specific metrics to quantify traversability compliance and elevation consistency, addressing the limitations of conventional on-road evaluation. Experiments on the ORAD-3D benchmark demonstrate that our approach reduces average trajectory error from 1.01m to 0.97m, improves traversability compliance from 0.621 to 0.644, and decreases elevation inconsistency from 0.428 to 0.322, highlighting the efficacy of action-aligned supervision and terrain-aware optimization for robust off-road driving.

</details>


### [85] [FingerViP: Learning Real-World Dexterous Manipulation with Fingertip Visual Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21331)
*Zhen Zhang,Weinan Wang,Hejia Sun,Qingpeng Ding,Xiangyu Chu,Guoxin Fang,K. W. Samuel Au*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FingerViP是一种利用指尖视觉感知的灵巧操作学习系统，通过在多指手每个手指上嵌入微型摄像头获得多视角反馈，并结合扩散模型的全身视觉运动策略，在多种复杂真实世界任务中取得80.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前灵巧操作通常依赖单一腕部安装的视角，容易受到遮挡，限制了需要多视角感知的任务性能。为克服这一局限，本文提出利用指尖视觉来提供全面、多视角的反馈。

Method: 设计了一种视觉增强的指尖模块，每个指尖嵌入微型摄像头；构建基于扩散模型的全身视觉运动策略，以第三视角相机和多视角指尖视觉为条件；每个指尖视觉特征增强对应的相机姿态编码和每指关节电流编码，以改善视角-本体感知对齐和接触感知。

Result: 在多种挑战性真实世界任务（如限位盒内按按钮、不稳定支撑中取棍、遮挡帘后取物、长时程开柜取物）上验证了有效性，总体成功率达80.8%。

Conclusion: 多视角指尖视觉能显著提升灵巧操作系统的感知能力和任务性能，FingerViP展示了良好的鲁棒性和适应性，所有硬件设计和代码将完全开源。

Abstract: The current practice of dexterous manipulation generally relies on a single wrist-mounted view, which is often occluded and limits performance on tasks requiring multi-view perception. In this work, we present FingerViP, a learning system that utilizes a visuomotor policy with fingertip visual perception for dexterous manipulation. Specifically, we design a vision-enhanced fingertip module with an embedded miniature camera and install the modules on each finger of a multi-fingered hand. The fingertip cameras substantially improve visual perception by providing comprehensive, multi-view feedback of both the hand and its surrounding environment. Building on the integrated fingertip modules, we develop a diffusion-based whole-body visuomotor policy conditioned on a third-view camera and multi-view fingertip vision, which effectively learns complex manipulation skills directly from human demonstrations. To improve view-proprioception alignment and contact awareness, each fingertip visual feature is augmented with its corresponding camera pose encoding and per-finger joint-current encoding. We validate the effectiveness of the multi-view fingertip vision and demonstrate the robustness and adaptability of FingerViP on various challenging real-world tasks, including pressing buttons inside a confined box, retrieving sticks from an unstable support, retrieving objects behind an occluding curtain, and performing long-horizon cabinet opening and object retrieval, achieving an overall success rate of 80.8%. All hardware designs and code will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [86] [PREVENT-JACK: Context Steering for Swarms of Long Heavy Articulated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21337)
*Adrian Baruck,Michael Dubé,Christoph Steup,Sanaz Mostaghim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Prevent-Jack方法，将稀疏覆盖的上下文引导框架应用于由长铰接车辆（HAVs）组成的群机器人系统，通过融合六种局部行为来防止折叠和碰撞，并通过15000次仿真验证了死锁和活锁在更大群体和更密集场景中更频繁发生的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群机器人研究多采用质点状机器人模型，而真实世界中大量存在长铰接车辆（HAVs），它们具有运动学约束、细长结构和铰接特性，带来独特的控制挑战。本文旨在将群机器人研究扩展到这类更真实的车辆模型上。

Method: 提出Prevent-Jack方法，引入稀疏覆盖的上下文引导框架，融合六种局部行为（包括关键的Evade Attraction行为），提供防折叠和防碰撞保证，但可能引入死锁和活锁。通过参数研究评估Evade Attraction行为的重要性，并利用15000次仿真进行性能评估。

Result: 死锁和活锁在更大群体和更密集场景中更频繁发生，峰值平均影响27%/31%的车辆。较大群体表现出更多等待行为，而较小群体则表现出更多规避行为。该方法支持多达十节拖车的车辆测试。

Conclusion: Prevent-Jack方法能有效协调长铰接车辆群，防止折叠和碰撞，但存在死锁和活锁的代价。未来需要进一步研究如何在保持防碰撞保证的同时减少死锁和活锁的发生。

Abstract: In this paper, we aim to extend the traditional point-mass-like robot representation in swarm robotics and instead study a swarm of long Heavy Articulated Vehicles (HAVs). HAVs are kinematically constrained, elongated, and articulated, introducing unique challenges. Local, decentralized coordination of these vehicles is motivated by many real-world applications. Our approach, Prevent-Jack, introduces the sparsely covered context steering framework in robotics. It fuses six local behaviors, providing guarantees against jackknifing and collisions at the cost of potential dead- and livelocks, tested for vehicles with up to ten trailers. We highlight the importance of the Evade Attraction behavior for deadlock prevention using a parameter study, and use 15,000 simulations to evaluate the swarm performance. Our extensive experiments and the results show that both the dead- and livelocks occur more frequently in larger swarms and denser scenarios, affecting a peak average of 27%/31% of vehicles. We observe that larger swarms exhibit increased waiting, while smaller swarms show increased evasion.

</details>


### [87] [Learn Weightlessness: Imitate Non-Self-Stabilizing Motions on Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21351)
*Yucheng Xin,Jiacheng Bao,Haoran Yang,Wenqiang Que,Dong Wang,Junbo Tan,Xueqian Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受生物启发的“失重机制”（Weightlessness Mechanism, WM），通过自动标注非自稳定运动中的关节放松策略，使人形机器人能够在与环境物理交互时灵活执行任务，在无需特定任务调优的情况下实现跨不同环境配置的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人全身控制方法在环境依赖型任务中表现有限，通常强制刚性轨迹跟踪而忽略了与环境的物理交互，缺乏像人类那样利用“失重”状态放松关节来借助环境接触稳定身体的机制。

Method: 设计了失重状态自动标注策略用于数据集标注，并提出失重机制（WM），动态决定放松哪些关节及放松程度，使机器人在执行目标运动的同时与环境有效交互。

Result: 在三种非自稳定任务（坐不同高度的椅子、躺在不同倾斜度的床上、靠墙）上，在仿真和Unitree G1机器人上验证了该方法在无需任务特定调优的情况下，能泛化到多种环境配置并保持运动稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了精确轨迹跟踪与自适应环境交互之间的差距，为接触丰富的人形机器人控制提供了一种生物启发的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of imitation and reinforcement learning has enabled remarkable advances in humanoid whole-body control, facilitating diverse human-like behaviors. However, research on environment-dependent motions remains limited. Existing methods typically enforce rigid trajectory tracking while neglecting physical interactions with the environment. We observe that humans naturally exploit a "weightless" state during non-self-stabilizing (NSS) motions--selectively relaxing specific joints to allow passive body--environment contact, thereby stabilizing the body and completing the motion. Inspired by this biological mechanism, we design a weightlessness-state auto-labeling strategy for dataset annotation; and we propose the Weightlessness Mechanism (WM), a method that dynamically determines which joints to relax and to what level, together enabling effective environmental interaction while executing target motions. We evaluate our approach on 3 representative NSS tasks: sitting on chairs of varying heights, lying down on beds with different inclinations, and leaning against walls via shoulder or elbow. Extensive experiments in simulation and on the Unitree G1 robot demonstrate that our WM method, trained on single-action demonstrations without any task-specific tuning, achieves strong generalization across diverse environmental configurations while maintaining motion stability. Our work bridges the gap between precise trajectory tracking and adaptive environmental interaction, offering a biologically-inspired solution for contact-rich humanoid control.

</details>


### [88] [RPG: Robust Policy Gating for Smooth Multi-Skill Transitions in Humanoid Fighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21355)
*Yucheng Xin,Jiacheng Bao,Yubo Dong,Xueqian Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li,Junbo Tan,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RPG混合专家策略框架，通过运动过渡随机化和时间随机化训练统一策略，实现人形机器人在长时间动态格斗中多技能间的平滑稳定过渡，并在仿真和Unitree G1机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人格斗技能学习方法（多单技能策略切换或通用策略模仿参考运动）在技能过渡时存在初始/终端状态不匹配导致的域外扰动，产生不平稳或不稳定行为，缺乏长时间动态格斗所需的高敏捷性和稳定性。

Method: 提出RPG混合专家策略框架，包含：(1) 运动过渡随机化与时间随机化，训练统一策略实现敏捷格斗动作的稳定性与平滑过渡；(2) 将行走/跑步运动与格斗技能集成的控制管线，支持任意时长的类人连续战斗，可随时无缝中断或切换策略。

Result: 仿真实验证明了框架的有效性，并在Unitree G1人形机器人上进行了真实世界部署，验证了其鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: RPG框架有效解决了人形机器人多技能平滑过渡的难题，实现了稳定、敏捷的长时间动态格斗，具备从仿真到真实环境的迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have demonstrated impressive motor skills in a wide range of tasks, yet whole-body control for humanlike long-time, dynamic fighting remains particularly challenging due to the stringent requirements on agility and stability. While imitation learning enables robots to execute human-like fighting skills, existing approaches often rely on switching among multiple single-skill policies or employing a general policy to imitate input reference motions. These strategies suffer from instability when transitioning between skills, as the mismatch of initial and terminal states across skills or reference motions introduces out-of-domain disturbances, resulting in unsmooth or unstable behaviors. In this work, we propose RPG, a hybrid expert policy framework, for smooth and stable humanoid multi-skills transition. Our approach incorporates motion transition randomization and temporal randomization to train a unified policy that generates agile fighting actions with stability and smoothness during skill transitions. Furthermore, we design a control pipeline that integrates walking/running locomotion with fighting skills, allowing humanlike long-time combat of arbitrary duration that can be seamlessly interrupted or transit action policies at any time. Extensive experiments in simulation demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, and real-world deployment on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot further validates its robustness and applicability.

</details>


### [89] [A Deployable Embodied Vision-Language Navigation System with Hierarchical Cognition and Context-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21363)
*Kuan Xu,Ruimeng Liu,Yizhuo Yang,Denan Liang,Tongxing Jin,Shenghai Yuan,Chen Wang,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可部署的具身视觉语言导航系统，通过将系统解耦为实时感知、记忆整合和推理三个异步模块，并构建认知记忆图结合加权旅行修理工问题优化，在资源受限硬件上实现了高效且鲁棒的高层推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法在强推理能力与实际平台高效部署之间存在根本性权衡，难以同时满足计算、内存、能耗和实时执行等严格约束。

Method: 将系统解耦为三个异步模块：实时感知模块（连续环境感知）、记忆整合模块（空间语义聚合）和推理模块（高层决策）。增量构建认知记忆图编码场景信息并分解为子图供VLM推理，同时将探索问题建模为上下文感知的加权旅行修理工问题以最小化视点加权等待时间。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人平台上的大量实验表明，该方法在导航成功率和效率上均优于现有VLN方法，同时在资源受限硬件上保持了实时性能。

Conclusion: 通过异步解耦架构和认知记忆图结合WTRP优化，成功弥合了具身智能与嵌入式部署之间的鸿沟，实现了效率与推理能力的兼顾。

Abstract: Bridging the gap between embodied intelligence and embedded deployment remains a key challenge in intelligent robotic systems, where perception, reasoning, and planning must operate under strict constraints on computation, memory, energy, and real-time execution. In vision-language navigation (VLN), existing approaches often face a fundamental trade-off between strong reasoning capabilities and efficient deployment on real-world platforms. In this paper, we present a deployable embodied VLN system that achieves both high efficiency and robust high-level reasoning on real-world robotic platforms. To achieve this, we decouple the system into three asynchronous modules: a real-time perception module for continuous environment sensing, a memory integration module for spatial-semantic aggregation, and a reasoning module for high-level decision making. We incrementally construct a cognitive memory graph to encode scene information, which is further decomposed into subgraphs to enable reasoning with a vision-language model (VLM). To further improve navigation efficiency and accuracy, we also leverage the cognitive memory graph to formulate the exploration problem as a context-aware Weighted Traveling Repairman Problem (WTRP), which minimizes the weighted waiting time of viewpoints. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world robotic platforms demonstrate improved navigation success and efficiency over existing VLN approaches, while maintaining real-time performance on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [90] [A Replicable Robotics Awareness Method Using LLM-Enabled Robotics Interaction: Evidence from a Corporate Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21377)
*S. A. Prieto,M. A. Gopee,Y. Ben Arab,B. García de Soto,J. Esteba,P. Olivera Brizzio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于挑战的机器人意识提升方法，通过LLM驱动的人形机器人活动在AD Ports Group员工中实施。102份问卷结果显示该方法在满意度（8.46/10）、兴趣提升（4.47/5）和协作理解（4.45/5）方面表现良好，但可靠性和可预测性评分较低，指出了技术改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM越来越多地被探索作为人与机器人系统的交互接口，但缺乏证据表明这些技术如何在真实组织环境中不仅用于交互，还能作为向非专业用户系统化介绍机器人技术的结构化手段。

Method: 在AD Ports Group（阿联酋）员工中实施LLM驱动的类人机器人活动，采用基于挑战的团队角色扮演方式，参与者通过语音命令与机器人交互，完成物流启发式任务。通过为期16天的后测问卷收集102份反馈进行量化评估。

Result: 整体满意度8.46/10；对机器人和AI兴趣提升4.47/5；对人机协作理解提升4.45/5；直接交互者报告自然交互4.37/5；交互随活动推进变得更易用4.74/5；但可靠性和可预测性评分较低。

Conclusion: 基于挑战的LLM驱动人形交互可作为工业运营环境中机器人意识提升的一种有前景、可复制的方法，但可靠性和可预测性方面的技术挑战有待后续改进。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly being explored as interfaces between humans and robotic systems, yet there remains limited evidence on how such technologies can be used not only for interaction, but also as a structured means of introducing robotics to non-specialist users in real organizational settings. This paper introduces and evaluates a challenge-based method for robotics awareness, implemented through an LLM-enabled humanoid robot activity conducted with employees of AD Ports Group in the United Arab Emirates. In the event, participants engaged with a humanoid robot in a logistics-inspired task environment using voice commands interpreted through an LLM-based control framework. The activity was designed as a team-based, role-driven experience intended to expose participants to embodied AI and human-robot collaboration without requiring prior robotics expertise. To evaluate the approach, a post-event survey remained open for 16 days and collected 102 responses. Results indicate strong overall reception, with high satisfaction (8.46/10), increased interest in robotics and AI (4.47/5), and improved understanding of emerging forms of human-robot collaboration (4.45/5). Participants who interacted directly with the robot also reported natural interaction (4.37/5) and a strong sense that interaction became easier as the activity progressed (4.74/5). At the same time, lower ratings for reliability and predictability point to important technical and design challenges for future iterations. The findings suggest that challenge-based, LLM-enabled humanoid interaction can serve as a promising and replicable method for robotics awareness in industrial and operational environments.

</details>


### [91] [From Noise to Intent: Anchoring Generative VLA Policies with Residual Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21391)
*Yiming Zhong,Yaoyu He,Zemin Yang,Pengfei Tian,Yifan Huang,Qingqiu Huang,Xinge Zhu,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResVLA提出了一种“从意图精炼”的新范式，通过频谱分析将机器人控制解耦为确定性低频锚点和随机高频残差，利用残差扩散桥在意图锚点上精炼局部动态，解决了具身智能中高层语义理解与低层物理控制之间的时空尺度错配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式VLA策略采用“从噪声生成”范式，忽视了认知与动作之间根本性的时空尺度错配，导致表示效率低下和优化过程中的条件对齐弱。

Method: 利用频谱分析将机器人运动解耦为全局意图（确定性低频锚点）和局部动态（随机高频残差），通过残差扩散桥在预测的意图锚点上精炼局部动态，实现从意图精炼而非从噪声生成的范式转变。

Result: 在仿真实验中达到竞争性性能，对语言和机器人本体扰动具有强鲁棒性，收敛速度比标准生成基线更快；在真实世界机器人实验中同样表现强劲。

Conclusion: ResVLA通过解耦控制和残差扩散桥有效弥合了高层语义理解与低层物理控制之间的差距，为具身智能中的视觉-语言-动作策略提供了更高效、更鲁棒的新范式。

Abstract: Bridging high-level semantic understanding with low-level physical control remains a persistent challenge in embodied intelligence, stemming from the fundamental spatiotemporal scale mismatch between cognition and action. Existing generative VLA policies typically adopt a "Generation-from-Noise" paradigm, which disregards this disparity, leading to representation inefficiency and weak condition alignment during optimization. In this work, we propose ResVLA, an architecture that shifts the paradigm to "Refinement-from-Intent." Recognizing that robotic motion naturally decomposes into global intent and local dynamics, ResVLA utilizes spectral analysis to decouple control into a deterministic low-frequency anchor and a stochastic high-frequency residual. By anchoring the generative process on the predicted intent, our model focuses strictly on refining local dynamics via a residual diffusion bridge. Extensive simulation experiments show that ResVLA achieves competitive performance, strong robustness to language and robot embodiment perturbations, and faster convergence than standard generative baselines. It also demonstrates strong performance in real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [92] [Ufil: A Unified Framework for Infrastructure-based Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21471)
*Simon Schäfer,Lucas Hegerath,Marius Molz,Massimo Marcon,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ufil是一个面向基础设施协同定位的统一框架，提供标准化的对象模型和可复用的多目标跟踪组件，支持异构数据源融合（车载ITS-G5消息、激光雷达、路面传感器），在仿真和实际测试平台上均实现车道级定位精度（横向RMSE约0.3m）和低于100ms的端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础设施定位系统开发受限于碎片化、应用特定的技术栈，感知、跟踪和中间件高度耦合，导致研究难以复用和独立改进单一模块。

Method: 提出Ufil统一框架，包含标准化对象模型和可复用多目标跟踪组件，提供预测、检测、关联、状态更新和轨迹管理的接口与参考实现；集成三种异构数据源（车载ITS-G5 CAM消息、激光雷达路侧节点、路面敏感层）到单一定位管线。

Result: 在三车道高速公路场景中（仿真423辆车、测试台355辆车），融合系统实现车道级横向精度：CARLA中横向RMSE为0.31m，CPM Lab中为0.29m，平均绝对朝向误差约2.2°；所有模态的中位端到端延迟低于100ms。

Conclusion: Ufil通过解耦的模块化架构和标准化接口，使研究人员能独立改进单个组件而无需重写整个管线，其跨尺度执行模型在仿真和真实测试台上表现一致，验证了框架的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Infrastructure-based localization enhances road safety and traffic management by providing state estimates of road users. Development is hindered by fragmented, application-specific stacks that tightly couple perception, tracking, and middleware. We introduce Ufil, a Unified Framework for Infrastructure-Based Localization with a standardized object model and reusable multi-object tracking components. Ufil offers interfaces and reference implementations for prediction, detection, association, state update, and track management, allowing researchers to improve components without reimplementing the pipeline. Ufil is open-source C++/ROS 2 software with documentation and executable examples. We demonstrate Ufil by integrating three heterogeneous data sources into a single localization pipeline combining (i) vehicle onboard units broadcasting ETSI ITS-G5 Cooperative Awareness Messages, (ii) a lidar-based roadside sensor node, and (iii) an in-road sensitive surface layer. The pipeline runs unchanged in the CARLA simulator and a small-scale CAV testbed, demonstrating Ufil's scale-independent execution model. In a three-lane highway scenario with 423 and 355 vehicles in simulation and testbed, respectively, the fused system achieves lane-level lateral accuracy with mean lateral position RMSEs of 0.31 m in CARLA and 0.29 m in the CPM Lab, and mean absolute orientation errors around 2.2°. Median end-to-end latencies from sensing to fused output remain below 100 ms across all modalities in both environments.

</details>


### [93] [MISTY: High-Throughput Motion Planning via Mixer-based Single-step Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21489)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Yiqian Tu,Zhiyuan Liu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MISTY提出了一种基于MLP-Mixer和单步推理的高吞吐量生成式运动规划器，通过潜在空间漂移损失将分布演化转移至训练阶段，在nuPlan基准上达到SOTA性能，推理速度超过99 FPS，较迭代扩散规划器快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的运动规划器由于需要迭代神经函数评估而导致高推理延迟，难以满足自动驾驶对实时性的严苛要求。

Method: 集成向量化子图编码器捕获环境上下文、变分自编码器将专家轨迹压缩至32维潜在流形、超轻量MLP-Mixer解码器消除二次注意力复杂度，并引入潜在空间漂移损失，通过显式吸引力和排斥力将分布演化完全转移至训练阶段。

Result: 在nuPlan Test14-hard数据集上，非反应和反应设置下综合分数分别达到80.32和82.21，推理速度超过99 FPS，端到端延迟仅10.1ms。

Conclusion: MISTY通过单步推理实现了与迭代扩散规划器相媲美乃至更优的生成质量，同时推理速度提升一个数量级，验证了将复杂分布演化转移至训练阶段的可行性。

Abstract: Multi-modal trajectory generation is essential for safe autonomous driving, yet existing diffusion-based planners suffer from high inference latency due to iterative neural function evaluations. This paper presents MISTY (Mixer-based Inference for Single-step Trajectory-drifting Yield), a high-throughput generative motion planner that achieves state-of-the-art closed-loop performance with pure single-step inference. MISTY integrates a vectorized Sub-Graph encoder to capture environment context, a Variational Autoencoder to structure expert trajectories into a compact 32-dimensional latent manifold, and an ultra-lightweight MLP-Mixer decoder to eliminate quadratic attention complexity. Importantly, we introduce a latent-space drifting loss that shifts the complex distribution evolution entirely to the training phase. By formulating explicit attractive and repulsive forces, this mechanism empowers the model to synthesize novel, proactive maneuvers, such as active overtaking, that are virtually absent from the raw expert demonstrations. Extensive evaluations on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that MISTY achieves state-of-the-art results on the challenging Test14-hard split, with comprehensive scores of 80.32 and 82.21 in non-reactive and reactive settings, respectively. Operating at over 99 FPS with an end-to-end latency of 10.1 ms, MISTY offers an order-of-magnitude speedup over iterative diffusion planners while while achieving significantly robust generation.

</details>


### [94] [X2-N: A Transformable Wheel-legged Humanoid Robot with Dual-mode Locomotion and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21541)
*Yan Ning,Xingzhou Chen,Delong Li,Hao Zhang,Hanfu Gai,Tongyuan Li,Cheng Zhang,Zhihui Peng,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高自由度可变形态机器人X2-N，支持人形与轮腿双模式及无缝转换，并设计了基于强化学习的全身控制框架，在动态滑行、爬楼梯和包裹递送等任务中展现出高效运动、强地形适应性和稳定的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮腿机器人通常采用固定轮足和有限的髋关节自由度，导致腿部运动时稳定性和机动性不足，且大多数平台缺少完整上肢，限制了灵巧操作能力。

Method: 设计了X2-N高自由度可变形态机器人，支持人形与轮腿双模式及无缝变换；提出基于强化学习的全身控制框架，实现混合运动、形态变换和操作的统一控制。

Result: 在动态滑行运动、爬楼梯和包裹递送等任务中验证了X2-N的高运动效率、强地形适应性和稳定的运动-操作协同性能。

Conclusion: X2-N在轮腿机器人领域取得了显著进展，展现了实际部署的潜力。

Abstract: Wheel-legged robots combine the efficiency of wheeled locomotion with the versatility of legged systems, enabling rapid traversal over both continuous and discrete terrains. However, conventional designs typically employ fixed wheels as feet and limited degrees of freedom (DoFs) at the hips, resulting in reduced stability and mobility during legged locomotion compared to humanoids with flat feet. In addition, most existing platforms lack a full upper body with arms, which limits their ability to perform dexterous manipulation tasks.
  In this letter, we present X2-N, a high-DoF transformable robot with dual-mode locomotion and manipulation. X2-N can operate in both humanoid and wheel-legged forms and transform seamlessly between them through joint reconfiguration. We further propose a reinforcement learning (RL)-based whole-body control framework tailored to this morphology, enabling unified control across hybrid locomotion, transformation, and manipulation. We validate X2-N in a range of challenging locomotion and manipulation tasks, including dynamic skating-like motion, stair climbing and package delivery. Results demonstrate high locomotion efficiency, strong terrain adaptability, and stable loco-manipulation performance of X2-N, highlighting its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [95] [SLAM as a Stochastic Control Problem with Partial Information: Optimal Solutions and Rigorous Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21693)
*Ilir Gusija,Fady Alajaji,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从最优随机控制视角重新审视主动SLAM问题，将其建模为部分信息下的决策问题，提出了一种新的探索阶段代价函数，并基于非标准POMDP推导出近似最优解，通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动SLAM是机器人领域的基础问题，现有研究缺乏从最优随机控制角度对该问题的严格数学分析和近似最优解的推导。

Method: 将主动SLAM重构为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），引入编码状态几何信息的探索阶段代价函数，在一般假设下分析正则性条件，推导出严格可证明的近似最优解。

Result: 建立了主动SLAM的随机控制框架，提出了新的几何感知代价函数，获得了理论上近似最优的解，并通过数值实验使用标准学习算法学习到了近似最优策略。

Conclusion: 该研究为主动SLAM问题提供了严格的理论基础和分析框架，其近似最优解和数值验证表明该方法在广泛机器人应用中具有实用潜力。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is a foundational state estimation problem in robotics in which a robot accurately constructs a map of its environment while also localizing itself within this construction. We study the active SLAM problem through the lens of optimal stochastic control, thereby recasting it as a decision-making problem under partial information. After reviewing several commonly studied models, we present a general stochastic control formulation of active SLAM together with a rigorous treatment of motion, sensing, and map representation. We introduce a new exploration stage cost that encodes the geometry of the state when evaluating information-gathering actions. This formulation, constructed as a nonstandard partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), is then analyzed to derive rigorously justified approximate solutions that are near-optimal. To enable this analysis, the associated regularity conditions are studied under general assumptions that apply to a wide range of robotics applications. For a particular case, we conduct an extensive numerical study in which standard learning algorithms are used to learn near-optimal policies.

</details>


### [96] [A Compact Peristaltic Pump Based on Magneto-Elastic Hysteresis with Single Pneumatic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21729)
*Minjo Park,Metin Sitti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅需单一气动输入和内置被动磁铁即可实现蠕动运动的软膜泵，通过建模、数值模拟和实验验证了其磁弹滞回行为与流体输运能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蠕动泵依赖机械、气动或磁驱动方式驱动膜变形，导致泵结构复杂、控制方案繁琐。本文旨在简化蠕动泵架构，实现更紧凑、易控制的软膜泵设计。

Method: 采用单一气动输入结合嵌入式被动磁铁驱动软膜产生蠕动运动；通过建模分析驱动机制与系统动力学，利用数值模拟预测内部流体流动，并通过实验验证磁弹滞回行为。

Result: 数值模拟成功预测了磁弹滞回行为，并通过概念验证原型实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的软膜泵在单一气动输入和被动磁铁驱动下可实现有效的蠕动运动，简化了传统蠕动泵的复杂结构，为柔性流体输运提供新的方案。

Abstract: Pumping fluids is fundamental to a wide range of industrial, environmental, and biomedical applications. Among various pumping mechanisms, peristaltic pumps enable efficient and safe fluid transport by deforming an elastic tube without direct contact with the working fluid. Although previous studies have introduced mechanical, pneumatic, or magnetic actuations to drive membrane deformation, these approaches often lead to complex pump architectures and control schemes. In this study, we present a soft membrane pump that achieves peristaltic motion through a single pneumatic input combined with an embedded passive magnet. The actuation mechanism and system dynamics were analyzed and simplified through modeling. Numerical simulations were conducted to predict the internal fluid flow, and the magneto-elastic hysteresis behavior observed in the simulations was successfully validated by experiments with a proof-of-concept prototype.

</details>


### [97] [Hi-WM: Human-in-the-World-Model for Scalable Robot Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21741)
*Yaxuan Li,Zhongyi Zhou,Yefei Chen,Yanjiang Guo,Jiaming Liu,Shanghang Zhang,Jianyu Chen,Yichen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Hi-WM框架，利用世界模型作为可复用的修正基板，让人在模型内部对失败轨迹进行干预和修正，无需真实机器人参与，显著提升策略性能。实验证明该方法优于基线和世界模型闭环基线，且世界模型评估与真实世界表现高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人后训练依赖真实物理执行，每次修正都需要机器人时间、场景设置、重置和操作员监督，成本高昂且低效。本文旨在将后训练从物理世界解放出来，利用世界模型作为可复用的修正基板。

Method: 1) 先在世界模型中闭环滚动策略；2) 当滚动出现错误或失败倾向时，人类直接在模型内提供短修正动作；3) Hi-WM缓存中间状态，支持回滚和分支，使单一失败状态可复用多次；4) 将修正后的轨迹加入训练集进行后训练。

Result: 在三项真实世界操作任务（涵盖刚体和可变形物体）及两种策略主干上，Hi-WM相比基础策略平均提升37.9个百分点的真实世界成功率，相比世界模型闭环基线提升19.0个百分点，且世界模型评估与真实世界表现相关系数r=0.953。

Conclusion: 世界模型不仅可以作为生成器或评估器，还可以作为机器人可扩展后训练的有效修正基板，减少对真实物理执行的依赖。

Abstract: Post-training is essential for turning pretrained generalist robot policies into reliable task-specific controllers, but existing human-in-the-loop pipelines remain tied to physical execution: each correction requires robot time, scene setup, resets, and operator supervision in the real world. Meanwhile, action-conditioned world models have been studied mainly for imagination, synthetic data generation, and policy evaluation. We propose \textbf{Human-in-the-World-Model (Hi-WM)}, a post-training framework that uses a learned world model as a reusable corrective substrate for failure-targeted policy improvement. A policy is first rolled out in closed loop inside the world model; when the rollout becomes incorrect or failure-prone, a human intervenes directly in the model to provide short corrective actions. Hi-WM caches intermediate states and supports rollback and branching, allowing a single failure state to be reused for multiple corrective continuations and yielding dense supervision around behaviors that the base policy handles poorly. The resulting corrective trajectories are then added back to the training set for post-training. We evaluate Hi-WM on three real-world manipulation tasks spanning both rigid and deformable object interaction, and on two policy backbones. Hi-WM improves real-world success by 37.9 points on average over the base policy and by 19.0 points over a world-model closed-loop baseline, while world-model evaluation correlates strongly with real-world performance (r = 0.953). These results suggest that world models can serve not only as generators or evaluators, but also as effective corrective substrates for scalable robot post-training.

</details>


### [98] [Task-Driven Co-Design of Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21894)
*Maximilian Stralz,Meshal Alharbi,Yujun Huang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单调协同设计理论的组合式框架，用于任务驱动的异构多机器人系统协同设计，能够联合优化机器人设计、编队组成和规划，并在任务约束下保证最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于机器人设计、编队组成和规划等领域的孤立优化，缺乏跨领域、系统级的协同设计方法，难以综合考虑不同设计决策之间的权衡和任务需求。

Method: 基于单调协同设计理论，将机器人、编队、规划器、执行器和评估器抽象为具有良好定义接口的互联设计问题，接口与具体实现和任务无关，从而实现任务约束下的联合优化。

Result: 通过系列案例研究，框架能够无缝集成新机器人类型、任务配置和概率感知目标，系统性地发现非显而易见的备选设计方案，并具备最优性保证，展示了灵活性、可扩展性和可解释性。

Conclusion: 该形式化协同设计框架为复杂异构多机器人系统提供了原则性的推理工具，能够有效实现跨域决策的系统级优化。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent robotic systems requires reasoning across tightly coupled decisions spanning heterogeneous domains, including robot design, fleet composition, and planning. Much effort has been devoted to isolated improvements in these domains, whereas system-level co-design considering trade-offs and task requirements remains underexplored. In this work, we present a formal and compositional framework for the task-driven co-design of heterogeneous multi-robot systems. Building on a monotone co-design theory, we introduce general abstractions of robots, fleets, planners, executors, and evaluators as interconnected design problems with well-defined interfaces that are agnostic to both implementations and tasks. This structure enables efficient joint optimization of robot design, fleet composition, and planning under task-specific performance constraints. A series of case studies demonstrates the capabilities of the framework. Various component models can be seamlessly incorporated, including new robot types, task profiles, and probabilistic sensing objectives, while non-obvious design alternatives are systematically uncovered with optimality guarantees. The results highlight the flexibility, scalability, and interpretability of the proposed approach, and illustrate how formal co-design enables principled reasoning about complex heterogeneous multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [99] [VistaBot: View-Robust Robot Manipulation via Spatiotemporal-Aware View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21914)
*Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yating Feng,Xiang Li,Yilun Chen,Pengfei Li,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VistaBot是一种将前馈几何模型与视频扩散模型集成的框架，用于实现视角鲁棒的闭环机器人操作，无需测试时的相机标定。通过4D几何估计、视角合成潜在提取和潜在动作学习三大组件，在ACT和π₀策略上分别提升视角泛化评分2.79倍和2.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端机器人操作模型在固定相机训练下对相机视角变化缺乏鲁棒性，限制了其在真实场景中的泛化能力。

Method: VistaBot包含三个核心组件：(1) 4D几何估计，(2) 视角合成潜在提取，(3) 潜在动作学习。框架将几何感知的合成模型与视频扩散模型集成，并分别集成到动作分块（ACT）和基于扩散的策略（π₀）中。

Result: 在ACT和π₀策略上，VistaBot分别将视角泛化评分（VGS）提升了2.79倍和2.63倍，同时实现了高质量的新视角合成。

Conclusion: VistaBot通过几何感知的合成模型、潜在动作规划器、新基准指标以及跨多种环境的广泛验证，有效解决了端到端操作模型对相机视角变化的鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Recently, end-to-end robotic manipulation models have gained significant attention for their generalizability and scalability. However, they often suffer from limited robustness to camera viewpoint changes when training with a fixed camera. In this paper, we propose VistaBot, a novel framework that integrates feed-forward geometric models with video diffusion models to achieve view-robust closed-loop manipulation without requiring camera calibration at test time. Our approach consists of three key components: 4D geometry estimation, view synthesis latent extraction, and latent action learning. VistaBot is integrated into both action-chunking (ACT) and diffusion-based ($π_0$) policies and evaluated across simulation and real-world tasks. We further introduce the View Generalization Score (VGS) as a new metric for comprehensive evaluation of cross-view generalization. Results show that VistaBot improves VGS by 2.79$\times$ and 2.63$\times$ over ACT and $π_0$, respectively, while also achieving high-quality novel view synthesis. Our contributions include a geometry-aware synthesis model, a latent action planner, a new benchmark metric, and extensive validation across diverse environments. The code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Architecture of an AI-Based Automated Course of Action Generation System for Military Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20862)
*Ji-il Park,Inwook Shim,Chong Hui Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对未来战争中行动方案（CoA）自动化规划系统的需求，基于公开信息梳理了相关军事学说，并提出了适用于CoA规划各阶段的AI技术及系统架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着作战速度、监视范围和武器射程的增加，传统人工CoA规划难以适应，亟需开发AI驱动的自动化CoA规划系统，但相关技术细节因安全限制而公开甚少，难以评估发展现状。

Method: 在公开信息范围内梳理相关军事学说，分析CoA规划各阶段特点，并对应提出适用的AI技术，最终设计自动化CoA规划系统的架构。

Result: 提出了适用于CoA规划各阶段的AI技术方案，以及一个自动化CoA规划系统的整体架构。

Conclusion: 本研究所提架构和技术可为未来AI驱动的自动化CoA规划系统开发提供参考。

Abstract: The automation system for Course of Action (CoA) planning is an essential element in future warfare. As maneuver speeds increase, surveillance ranges extend, and weapon ranges grow, the operational area expands, making traditional manned-based CoA planning increasingly challenging. Consequently, the development of an AI-based automated CoA planning system is becoming increasingly necessary. Accordingly, several countries and defense organizations are actively developing AI-based CoA planning systems. However, due to security restrictions and limited public disclosure, the technical maturity of such systems remains difficult to assess. Furthermore, as these systems are military-related, their details are not publicly disclosed, making it difficult to accurately assess the current level of development. In response to this, this study aims to introduce relevant doctrines within the scope of publicly available information and present applicable AI technologies for each stage of the CoA planning process. Ultimately, it proposes an architecture for the development of an automated CoA planning system.

</details>


### [101] [Escaping the Agreement Trap: Defensibility Signals for Evaluating Rule-Governed AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20972)
*Michael O'Herlihy,Rosa Català*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出在规则治理环境下，内容审核评估使用人工标签一致性存在"一致性陷阱"缺陷，提出以策略为基础的正确性评估框架，包括可辩护性指数(DI)、模糊性指数(AI)和概率性可辩护信号(PDS)。在19.3万Reddit审核决策上验证，发现一致性指标与策略基础指标存在33-46.6个百分点的差距，且79.8-80.6%的模型假阴性实为策略合理的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内容审核系统通过衡量与人工标签的一致性来评估性能，但在规则治理环境下，同一政策可能支持多个逻辑一致的决策，一致性指标惩罚了合理决策，误将歧义性表征为错误，即"一致性陷阱"。

Method: 形式化定义了策略基础的正确性评估框架，提出可辩护性指数(DI)和模糊性指数(AI)；引入概率性可辩护信号(PDS)，通过审计模型token对数概率估计推理稳定性；利用LLM推理轨迹作为治理信号，审计模型不直接决定内容是否违规，而是验证决策是否可从规则层次结构中逻辑推导。

Result: 在19.3万Reddit审核决策上验证，一致性指标与策略基础指标存在33-46.6个百分点差距；79.8-80.6%的模型假阴性实为策略合理决策；规则具体化可将AI降低10.8个百分点；PDS方差主要源于治理歧义而非解码噪声；基于这些信号的Governance Gate实现78.6%自动化覆盖率与64.9%风险降低。

Conclusion: 规则治理环境下的评估应从与历史标签的一致性转向基于明确规则的推理有效性评估。

Abstract: Content moderation systems are typically evaluated by measuring agreement with human labels. In rule-governed environments this assumption fails: multiple decisions may be logically consistent with the governing policy, and agreement metrics penalize valid decisions while mischaracterizing ambiguity as error - a failure mode we term the Agreement Trap. We formalize evaluation as policy-grounded correctness and introduce the Defensibility Index (DI) and Ambiguity Index (AI). To estimate reasoning stability without additional audit passes, we introduce the Probabilistic Defensibility Signal (PDS), derived from audit-model token logprobs. We harness LLM reasoning traces as a governance signal rather than a classification output by deploying the audit model not to decide whether content violates policy, but to verify whether a proposed decision is logically derivable from the governing rule hierarchy. We validate the framework on 193,000+ Reddit moderation decisions across multiple communities and evaluation cohorts, finding a 33-46.6 percentage-point gap between agreement-based and policy-grounded metrics, with 79.8-80.6% of the model's false negatives corresponding to policy-grounded decisions rather than true errors. We further show that measured ambiguity is driven by rule specificity: auditing 37,286 identical decisions under three tiers of the same community rules reduces AI by 10.8 pp while DI remains stable. Repeated-sampling analysis attributes PDS variance primarily to governance ambiguity rather than decoding noise. A Governance Gate built on these signals achieves 78.6% automation coverage with 64.9% risk reduction. Together, these results show that evaluation in rule-governed environments should shift from agreement with historical labels to reasoning-grounded validity under explicit rules.

</details>


### [102] [Value-Conflict Diagnostics Reveal Widespread Alignment Faking in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20995)
*Inderjeet Nair,Jie Ruan,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VLAF诊断框架，发现对齐伪装（alignment faking）比以往报告更普遍——即使7B参数模型也会出现（olmo2-7b-instruct达37%），并利用表示空间中的单一对比方向实现了无需标注数据的轻量级缓解方法，将伪装率降低85.8%、94.0%和57.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐伪装诊断依赖高毒性、明确有害的场景，导致模型立即拒绝，无法考察模型对开发者策略、监控条件及不服从后果的深思熟虑过程，因而根本检测不到伪装倾向。亟需一种能避开拒绝行为、同时保留有意义的权衡情境的诊断方法。

Method: 提出VLAF框架，核心假设是对齐伪装最可能发生在开发者策略与模型强烈持有的价值观冲突时。使用道德上无歧义的场景来探查不同道德价值观下的冲突，绕过拒绝行为同时保留权衡空间。进一步发现监督条件诱导的激活偏移沿表示空间单一方向分布，利用这一发现构建对比引导向量，实现无需标注数据、计算开销极小的推理时缓解。

Result: 对齐伪装比先前报告普遍得多，在7B参数模型（olmo2-7b-instruct）中伪装率达37%；利用单一对比方向进行缓解，在olmo2-7b-instruct、olmo2-13b-instruct和qwen3-8b上分别实现85.8%、94.0%和57.7%的相对伪装率降低。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装现象比已有认知更广泛且规模更小；表示空间中对齐伪装的驱动机制可由单一方向刻画，该发现可用于高效缓解，无需额外标注数据。

Abstract: Alignment faking, where a model behaves aligned with developer policy when monitored but reverts to its own preferences when unobserved, is a concerning yet poorly understood phenomenon, in part because current diagnostic tools remain limited. Prior diagnostics rely on highly toxic and clearly harmful scenarios, causing most models to refuse immediately. As a result, models never deliberate over developer policy, monitoring conditions, or the consequences of non-compliance, making these diagnostics fundamentally unable to detect alignment faking propensity. To support study of this phenomenon, we first introduce VLAF, a diagnostic framework grounded in the hypothesis that alignment faking is most likely when developer policy conflicts with a model's strongly held values. VLAF uses morally unambiguous scenarios to probe this conflict across diverse moral values, bypassing refusal behavior while preserving meaningful deliberative stakes. Using VLAF, we find that alignment faking is substantially more prevalent than previously reported, occurring in models as small as 7B parameters - with olmo2-7b-instruct faking alignment in 37% of cases.Finally, we show that oversight conditions induce activation shifts that lie along a single direction in representation space. This means the behavioral divergence driving alignment faking can be captured by a single contrastive steering vector, which we exploit for lightweight inference-time mitigation. Finally, we exploit this for mitigation that requires no labeled data and minimal computational overhead, achieving relative reductions in alignment faking of 85.8%, 94.0%, and 57.7% on olmo2-7b-instruct, olmo2-13b-instruct, and qwen3-8b respectively.

</details>


### [103] [The Last Harness You'll Ever Build](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21003)
*Haebin Seong,Li Yin,Haoran Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个双层框架自动优化AI智能体的任务适配过程：第一层（Harness Evolution Loop）自动优化单个任务的智能体装备（提示词、工具、编排逻辑等），第二层（Meta-Evolution Loop）跨任务学习如何高效地进行这种优化，最终实现零人工干预的领域适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体在部署到复杂、领域特定的工作流时，每个新任务都需要专家手动设计prompt、工具、编排逻辑和评估标准，即"装备工程"（harness engineering）成本极高，严重阻碍了智能体的规模化扩展。

Method: 提出双层进化框架：第一层Harness Evolution Loop包含Worker Agent（执行任务）、Evaluator Agent（对抗性诊断失败并评分）、Evolution Agent（基于历史修改装备）；第二层Meta-Evolution Loop跨任务学习进化协议Λ，从而在新任务上实现快速装备收敛。

Result: 框架将人工装备工程转化为自动化装备工程，并进一步实现了对自动化本身的自动化设计——学习到的协议Λ^(best)能够使任何新任务的装备快速收敛，无需人工干预。

Conclusion: 本文形式化了该方法与元学习的对应关系，给出了两种算法，并展示了从"手动设计自动化系统"到"自动化地设计自动化的方法"的范式跃迁，显著降低了AI智能体在新型领域部署的人工成本。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed on complex, domain-specific workflows -- navigating enterprise web applications that require dozens of clicks and form fills, orchestrating multi-step research pipelines that span search, extraction, and synthesis, automating code review across unfamiliar repositories, and handling customer escalations that demand nuanced domain knowledge. \textbf{Each new task domain requires painstaking, expert-driven harness engineering}: designing the prompts, tools, orchestration logic, and evaluation criteria that make a foundation model effective. We present a two-level framework that automates this process. At the first level, the \textbf{Harness Evolution Loop} optimizes a worker agent's harness $\mathcal{H}$ for a single task: a Worker Agent $W_{\mathcal{H}}$ executes the task, an Evaluator Agent $V$ adversarially diagnoses failures and scores performance, and an Evolution Agent $E$ modifies the harness based on the full history of prior attempts. At the second level, the \textbf{Meta-Evolution Loop} optimizes the evolution protocol $Λ= (W_{\mathcal{H}}, \mathcal{H}^{(0)}, V, E)$ itself across diverse tasks, \textbf{learning a protocol $Λ^{(\text{best})}$ that enables rapid harness convergence on any new task -- so that adapting an agent to a novel domain requires no human harness engineering at all.} We formalize the correspondence to meta-learning and present both algorithms. The framework \textbf{shifts manual harness engineering into automated harness engineering}, and takes one step further -- \textbf{automating the design of the automation itself}.

</details>


### [104] [Adaptive Test-Time Compute Allocation with Evolving In-Context Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21018)
*Bowen Zuo,Dongruo Zhou,Yinglun Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应测试时计算分配框架，通过预热阶段识别简单查询并构建初始问答池，再通过自适应阶段聚焦未解决查询并利用上下文示例重塑生成分布，在数学、代码和推理基准上以更少计算量超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算扩展方法要么采用静态计算分配，要么从固定生成分布中采样，缺乏对计算位置和生成方式的联合自适应调整能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）预热阶段识别简单查询并从测试集自身构建初始问答对池；2）自适应阶段将计算集中在未解决查询上，通过语义相关的成功响应作为上下文示例来动态重塑生成分布，而非从固定分布重新采样。

Result: 在数学、代码和推理基准上，该方法持续优于现有基线，同时消耗显著更少的推理时计算。

Conclusion: 联合自适应计算分配与生成分布重塑是一种高效且有效的测试时计算扩展策略。

Abstract: While scaling test-time compute can substantially improve model performance, existing approaches either rely on static compute allocation or sample from fixed generation distributions. In this work, we introduce a test-time compute allocation framework that jointly adapts where computation is spent and how generation is performed. Our method begins with a warm-up phase that identifies easy queries and assembles an initial pool of question-response pairs from the test set itself. An adaptive phase then concentrates further computation on unresolved queries while reshaping their generation distributions through evolving in-context demonstrations -- conditioning each generation on successful responses from semantically related queries rather than resampling from a fixed distribution. Experiments across math, coding, and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines while consuming substantially less inference-time compute.

</details>


### [105] [HypEHR: Hyperbolic Modeling of Electronic Health Records for Efficient Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21027)
*Yuyu Liu,Sarang Rajendra Patil,Mengjia Xu,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双曲几何的紧凑型Lorentz模型HypEHR，用于电子健康记录问答任务，通过层次感知的预训练和几何一致的交叉注意力机制，在参数远少于LLM方案的情况下接近其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的EHR问答流水线部署成本高，且未能充分利用临床数据的层次结构（如ICD本体和患者轨迹在双曲空间中具有自然几何特性）。

Method: 构建紧凑的Lorentz空间模型，将诊断代码、就诊记录和问题嵌入双曲空间；采用几何一致的交叉注意力机制和类型特定的指针头进行答案预测；通过下一就诊诊断预测和层次感知正则化进行预训练，对齐ICD本体表示。

Result: 在两个MIMIC-IV基准上，HypEHR以远少于LLM方法的参数接近其问答性能。

Conclusion: 双曲几何可高效编码临床数据的层次结构，在参数效率上优于传统LLM流水线，为EHR问答提供了低成本替代方案。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) question answering is often handled by LLM-based pipelines that are costly to deploy and do not explicitly leverage the hierarchical structure of clinical data. Motivated by evidence that medical ontologies and patient trajectories exhibit hyperbolic geometry, we propose HypEHR, a compact Lorentzian model that embeds codes, visits, and questions in hyperbolic space and answers queries via geometry-consistent cross-attention with type-specific pointer heads. HypEHR is pretrained with next-visit diagnosis prediction and hierarchy-aware regularization to align representations with the ICD ontology. On two MIMIC-IV-based EHR-QA benchmarks, HypEHR approaches LLM-based methods while using far fewer parameters. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yuyuliu11037/HypEHR.

</details>


### [106] [Who Defines Fairness? Target-Based Prompting for Demographic Representation in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21036)
*Marzia Binta Nizam,James Davis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级、推理时的框架，通过提示层干预缓解文本到图像模型中的代表性偏差，允许用户选择多种公平性定义，无需重新训练或修改模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型（如Stable Diffusion、DALL-E）在生成图像时复现社会偏见，例如高地位职业输出更浅肤色、低地位职业输出更多样化肤色，强化刻板印象。现有缓解方法需要重训练或精心策划的数据集，对大多数用户不可及。

Method: 提出推理时轻量级框架，通过提示层干预缓解偏差。允许用户选择多种公平性规范（从均匀分布到LLM提供的更复杂定义），这些分布指导构建相应比例的、人口统计特征特定的提示变体。通过审计对声明目标的遵守度并测量实际肤色分布来评估对齐程度，而非假定均匀性即为"公平"。

Result: 在涵盖30个职业和6个非职业背景的36个提示上，该方法将观察到的肤色结果向声明目标方向转移，并且当目标直接在肤色空间定义时，减少了与目标的偏差。

Conclusion: 展示了公平性干预如何在推理时变得透明、可控和可操作，直接赋能生成式AI用户。

Abstract: Text-to-image(T2I) models like Stable Diffusion and DALL-E have made generative AI widely accessible, yet recent studies reveal that these systems often replicate societal biases, particularly in how they depict demographic groups across professions. Prompts such as 'doctor' or 'CEO' frequently yield lighter-skinned outputs, while lower-status roles like 'janitor' show more diversity, reinforcing stereotypes. Existing mitigation methods typically require retraining or curated datasets, making them inaccessible to most users. We propose a lightweight, inference-time framework that mitigates representational bias through prompt-level intervention without modifying the underlying model. Instead of assuming a single definition of fairness, our approach allows users to select among multiple fairness specifications-ranging from simple choices such as a uniform distribution to more complex definitions informed by a large language model(LLM) that cites sources and provides confidence estimates. These distributions guide the construction of demographic specific prompt variants in the corresponding proportions, and we evaluate alignment by auditing adherence to the declared target and measuring the resulting skin tone distribution rather than assuming uniformity as 'fairness'. Across 36 prompts spanning 30 occupations and 6 non-occupational contexts, our method shifts observed skin-tone outcomes in directions consistent with the declared target, and reduces deviation from targets when the target is defined directly in skin-tone space(fallback). This work demonstrates how fairness interventions can be made transparent, controllable, and usable at inference time, directly empowering users of generative AI.

</details>


### [107] [Active Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21044)
*Richard Arthur,Virginia DiDomizio,Louis Hoebel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主动数据（Active Data）的自底向上方法，用于推理大规模复杂数据集，并在空中交通流量管理领域进行了实现和性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂领域中，问题特定的分解方法相比整体式设计具有优势，有助于理解和规范化设计。本文旨在解决大规模复杂数据集上的推理问题，提供一种直观且易处理的方法。

Method: 基于主动数据（Active Data）的方法，将数据视为能够主动与环境交互的原子对象，采用自底向上的方式处理计算和概念复杂性。

Result: 在空中交通流量管理领域中实现了主动数据的基础概念，并讨论了该实现的性能表现。

Conclusion: 主动数据方法能够有效改善面对计算和概念复杂性时的设计，为复杂数据集的推理提供了一种直观且可行的途径。

Abstract: In some complex domains, certain problem-specific decompositions can provide advantages over monolithic designs by enabling comprehension and specification of the design. In this paper we present an intuitive and tractable approach to reasoning over large and complex data sets. Our approach is based on Active Data, i.e., data as atomic objects that actively interact with environments. We describe our intuition about how this bottom-up approach improves designs confronting computational and conceptual complexity. We describe an implementation of the base Active Data concepts within the air traffic flow management domain and discuss performance for this implementation.

</details>


### [108] [InVitroVision: a Multi-Modal AI Model for Automated Description of Embryo Development using Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21061)
*Nicklas Neu,Thomas Ebner,Jasmin Primus,Raphael Zefferer,Bernhard Schenkenfelder,Mathias Brunbauer,Florian Kromp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过微调多模态视觉语言模型PaliGemma-2，仅用1000张胚胎图像及其标注，开发了InVitroVision模型，用于预测胚胎形态和发育的自然语言描述。该模型在IVF任务上优于ChatGPT 5.2和基础模型，证明了基础视觉语言模型在有限数据下可泛化至IVF领域，有望推动大语言模型在辅助生殖中的信息检索和证据支持应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI在IVF中的应用虽能提升决策一致性和标准化，但通常依赖标注数据且未充分利用IVF数据的多模态特性。研究者探索利用基础视觉语言模型，通过少量数据微调实现胚胎形态和发育的自然语言描述预测，以突破现有方法对大量标注数据的依赖，并充分利用多模态信息。

Method: 使用公开的胚胎延时摄影数据集，微调多模态视觉语言模型PaliGemma-2，仅用1000张图像及其对应的胚胎形态、细胞周期和发育阶段的文本描述进行训练，构建了InVitroVision模型。

Result: InVitroVision在综合指标上优于商业模型ChatGPT 5.2和基础模型，且模型性能随训练数据集规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: 基础视觉语言模型在有限数据下能够泛化至IVF任务，实现胚胎形态和发育的自然语言描述预测。该方法可推动大语言模型从相关出版物和指南中检索信息与科学证据，并支持IVF中多个下游任务的少样本适应。

Abstract: The application of artificial intelligence (AI) in IVF has shown promise in improving consistency and standardization of decisions, but often relies on annotated data and does not make use of the multimodal nature of IVF data. We investigated whether foundational vision-language models can be fine-tuned to predict natural language descriptions of embryo morphology and development. Using a publicly available embryo time-lapse dataset, we fine-tuned PaliGemma-2, a multi-modal vision-language model, with only 1,000 images and corresponding captions, describing embryo morphology, embryonic cell cycle and developmental stage. Our results show that the fine-tuned model, InVitroVision, outperformed a commercial model, ChatGPT 5.2, and base models in overall metrics, with performance improving with larger training datasets. This study demonstrates the potential of foundational vision-language models to generalize to IVF tasks with limited data, enabling the prediction of natural language descriptions of embryo morphology and development.
